[471:58.72] error reaches a minimum value and after
[472:01.36] that when it tries to still increase the

[472:07.76] recognize that whenever it is increasing
[472:09.76] the weight after this point error is

[554:16.08] I mean to say here is that when we tend
[554:17.76] to work with a very huge data set and
[554:19.92] multiple RNN layer, we are at the risk
[554:22.16] of vanishing gradient problem. Now you
[554:24.32] might be wondering what is this
[554:25.44] vanishing gradient, right? Well, you see
[554:27.84] when training a very deep neural
[554:29.60] network, gradient or the derivatives
[554:31.68] decrease exponentially as it propagates
[554:33.68] down the layer. This is known as
[554:35.36] vanishing gradient problem. These
[554:37.36] gradients are actually used to update
[554:39.28] the weights of a neural network. But
[554:41.20] when the gradients vanish, these weights
[554:43.36] will not get updated. In the worst case
[554:45.60] scenario, it will completely stop the
[554:47.36] neural network from training. This
[554:49.12] vanishing gradient problem is a common
[554:51.04] issue in very deep neural networks. So
[554:53.68] to overcome this vanishing gradient
[554:55.44] problem in RNN's longsh short-term
[554:57.68] memory was introduced. You see LSTM or
[555:00.72] longshot memory is a modification to
[555:02.80] RNN's hidden layer. LSTM is capable of
[555:05.68] remembering RNN's weights and their
[555:07.68] inputs over a very long period of time.
[555:09.76] In LSTM in addition to the hidden state,
[555:12.24] cell state is passed down to the next
[555:14.00] block. The way LSTM works is that it can
[555:16.72] capture long range dependencies that is
[555:19.20] old weights. It can have memory of
[555:21.28] previous inputs for a very extended time
[555:23.36] duration. The way LSTM cell does this is
[555:26.00] by using three main gates. First one is
[555:28.16] a forget gate. Forget gate removes the
[555:30.56] information that is no longer useful in
[555:32.48] the cell state. Then we have input gate.
[555:35.28] Additional information to the cell state
[555:37.20] is added by input gate. And finally we
[555:39.76] have something called as output gate.
[555:41.44] Additional useful information to the
[555:43.04] cell state is also added by an output
[555:45.44] gate. This getting mechanism of LSTM has
[555:48.32] allowed network to learn the conditions
[555:50.40] for when to forget, ignore or keep
[555:52.48] information in the memory cell. So let
[555:55.04] me now quickly move to my Jupyter
[555:56.72] notebook and show you how I can
[555:58.24] implement LSTM or name entity
